So implement:
```
def c_arrays(h):
    n=len(h); dx=2/n; pos=np.empty(n); neg=np.empty(n)
    for s in range(n):
        L=n-s
        pos[s]=dx*np.dot(h[:L], 1-h[s:])
        # neg[0]=pos[0]; for s=0 same
        neg[s]=dx*np.dot(h[s:], 1-h[:L])
    return pos,neg
```
But O(n) Python loop per dot; n=256 okay. `1-h[s:]` creates array; can use `np.dot(h[:L], h[s:])`? Since ∑h_i(1-h_{i+s})=∑h_i -∑h_i h_{i+s}. Need mass partial? `np.dot(h[:L],1-h[s:])` fine. For gradient, need all c. Could compute c arrays using vectorized? Python loop with dot in C: n dots of varying length. For n=256, total 32k observations? Actually total operations n(n+1)/2 ~32k, fine. For n=1024 ~0.5M per call, maybe okay for few hundred but maybe 50 iterations 25M, fine. For 1000 iterations 500M, maybe 2-5s. Acceptable 1100s.

Projection: parent's `project_feasible` does iterative distribute to room; can be O(iter*n) up to 2000, heavy. Need better projection. We can implement exact projection to box + sum target efficiently:
Project y to {0≤x≤1, sum=target}. This is isotonic-like. We can find threshold θ such that x_i = clip(y_i - θ,0,1) and sum=target. Since target within [0,n]. Solve scalar monotone. Use bisection on θ. Or parent's iterative is okay but 2000*n per step maybe heavy. Implement efficient:
```
def project_feasible(y, target=None):
 n=len(y); target = sum_target default n/2
 lo,hi = min(y)-1, max(y)+1? Need find t such that sum clip(y-t)=target.
 for _ in range(60):
   mid=(lo+hi)/2; s=np.clip(y-mid,0,1).sum()
   if s>target: lo=mid else hi=mid
 x=np.clip(y-(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
 # adjust tiny residual by adding/subtracting smallest clipping? Diff ~1e-15.
 # To get exact sum, distribute residual among non-saturated values:
 diff=target-x.sum()
 if diff>0: room=1-x; idx where room>1e-12; x[idx]+=diff/room[idx].sum()*room[idx]
 else: base=x; idx where base>1e-12; x[idx]+=diff/base[idx].sum()*base[idx]
 return np.clip(x,0,1)
```
Need target passed. If target exactly n/2. This is O(60n) per projection, heavy but 60 maybe okay. We can use parent iterative maybe faster if y near feasible (few iterations). Compute gradient code carefully:
```
def smooth_grad_from_weights(c_full, weights, h, dx):
 n=len(h); grad=np.zeros(n)
 # zero shift at idx n-1
 w=weights[n-1]
 if w>0: grad += w*dx*(1.0 - 2.0*h)
 for s in range(1,n):
   wp=weights[n+s] # shift +s
   if wp>0:
      L=n-s
      grad[:L] += wp*dx*(1.0 - h[s:s+L])
      grad[s:s+L] -= wp*dx*h[:L]
   wn=weights[n-1-s] # shift -s
   if wn>0:
      L=n-s
      grad[s:s+L] += wn*dx*(1.0 - h[:L])
      grad[:L] -= wn*dx*h[s:s+L]
 return grad
```
Check signs:
For positive shift c=dx∑ h_i(1-h_{i+s}). Deriv wrt h_i: dx(1-h_{i+s}); wrt h_{i+s}: -dx h_i. Yes.
For negative shift c=dx∑ h_{i+s}(1-h_i). Deriv h_{i+s}: dx(1-h_i); h_i: -dx h_{i+s}. Yes.
For zero: c=dx∑h_i(1-h_i), deriv dx(1-2h).

Gradient of smooth F is weighted sum. Good.

def count_arrays(h):

 omh = 1.0-h

   L=n-s
   pos[s]=np.dot(h[:L], omh[s:])

  n=len(h); target=n/2
  best_h=h.copy(); best_f=smooth_f(h) maybe count f
  for it in range(max_iter):
    if time>deadline: break
    fg = smooth_f_grad(h)
    if grad_norm<1e-12 break
    # backtracking
    f=fg
    step=lr_init
    # Use direction -grad; maybe project original? gradient may have large mean; okay.
    accepted=False
    for _ in range(30):
       y=h - step*grad
       y=project(y,target)
       fy=smooth_f(y)
       if fy < f - 1e-12:
          h=y; f=fy; accepted=True; break
       step *=0.5
       if step<1e-6: break
    if not accepted:
       # try scaling direction? maybe break or use small
    if f - best_f > 1e-12: best...
    lr_init adjust
  return best
```

```

```

`count_full(h)` implementation optimized:
```
def count_full(h):
 n=len(h); pos=np.empty(n); neg=np.empty(n)
 omh = 1.0 - h
 # s=0
 pos[0]=np.dot(h, omh); neg[0]=pos[0]
 for s in range(1,n):
   L=n-s
   pos[s]=np.dot(h[:L], omh[s:])
   neg[s]=np.dot(h[s:], omh[:L])
 full=np.empty(2*n-1)
 full[:n-1]=neg[:0:-1]
 full[n-1]=neg[0]
 full[n:]=pos[1:]
 return full
```
Need for n=1? full length1. Use if n==1 return array([0]).

Potential memory: pos/neg arrays length n; fine. `np.dot` returns scalar; performance okay. We can avoid storing pos/neg? Build full directly:
```
full = np.empty(2*n-1); full[n-1]=dot(h,omh)
for s in range(1,n):
 L=n-s
 full[n+s]=dot(h[:L], omh[s:])
 full[n-1-s]=dot(h[s:], omh[:L])
return full
```
This is simpler and memory less. Use local `h_local=h; omh_local=omh`. Good.

Let's refine the smoothing and gradient for counts.

Implement `_smooth_fg(x)`:
```
def _smooth_fg(x):
    full = count_full(x)
    m = float(full.max())
    a = _ALPHA
    if a <= 1.0:
       f=m
       w = np.zeros_like(full); w[full == m] = 1.0/(full==m).sum()
    else:
       sh = a*(full - m)
       sm = sh.max()
       ez = np.exp(sh - sm)
       S = ez.sum()
       f = m + np.log(S)/a
       w = ez/S
    # gradient
    n_=len(x); grad=np.zeros(n_)
    # We need w_full: w already. n may be 1.
    w0 = float(w[n_-1])
    if w0 > 1e-15:
        grad += w0*(1.0 - 2.0*x)
    for s in range(1,n_):
       wp = float(w[n_+s])
       if wp > 1e-15:
          L=n_-s
          gmul = wp
          grad[:L] += gmul*(1.0 - x[s:s+L])
          grad[s:s+L] -= gmul*x[:L]
       wn = float(w[n_ - 1 - s])
       if wn > 1e-15:
          L=n_-s
          grad[s:s+L] += wn*(1.0 - x[:L])
          grad[:L] -= wn*x[s:s+L]
    return f, grad
```
For counts, no dx. Check weight length: full length 2n-1, indices 0..2n-2. zero n-1. positive s index n-1+s, works for s=1..n-1 max 2n-2. negative index n-1-s, for s=n-1 index0. Good.

If α<=1, smooth max not needed? Use exact max subgradient not differentiable; but minimize requires gradient. Use α at least 10. Good.

`_smooth_fun(x)` returns `_smooth_fg(x)[0]`; `_smooth_grad(x)`. Double count. We can optimize by implementing `_smooth_fun` to compute full and f, store weights? But not. Keep.

For L-BFGS penalty:
```
def _pen_fun(x):
    f,_ = _smooth_fg(x)
    diff = float(x.sum() - _TARGET)
    return f + 0.5*_PEN*diff*diff
def _pen_grad(x):
    _,g = _smooth_fg(x)
    diff = float(x.sum()-_TARGET)
    return g + _PEN*diff*np.ones(_N)
```
Double. Could optimize by inlining and using two count_full. OK.

SLSQP constraint jac:
```
def _eq_fun(x): return float(x.sum() - _TARGET)
def _eq_jac(x): return np.ones(_N)
```
Return new ones each call. Good.

Active subgradient polish with counts:
Use exact max, not smooth. Define `exact_max_subgrad(h)`:
- full=count_full(h); m=full.max(); active = full >= m - tol. Weights average over active (or top few) to reduce chatter.
- grad = weighted sum of exact gradients with uniform weights over active (weights=1/k). Not normalized? Direction; step size separate.
- Projected step with line search on exact max or smooth. This is like averaged subgradient.
```
def subgrad_polish(h,n,deadline,maxiter=200,lr_init=1.0,tol=0.5):
  best=h.copy(); bestC=max_count(h)*2/n
  lr=lr_init
  for it...
    full=count_full(h); m=full.max()
    active = np.where(full >= m - 1e-6)[0]
    # Use top 10 maybe to smooth
    if len(active)>20: idx=largest 20? Or uniform all if many. For max plateau, average helpful.
    w = 1.0/len(active_indices)
    grad = gradient_from_weights(full? weights uniform over active)

Detailed `project_feasible`:
```
def project_feasible(y, target=None):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
    n = len(y)
    if n == 0: return y
    if target is None: target = 0.5 * n
    # if close, adjust
    s = y.sum()
    diff = target - s
    if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
        return np.clip(y,0,1)
    # bisection if y outside or diff large
    lo = float(np.min(y)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(y)) + 1.0
    for _ in range(70):
        mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
        s = np.clip(y - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum()
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    theta = 0.5*(lo+hi)
    x = np.clip(y - theta, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - x.sum()
    tol=1e-12
    if abs(diff) > tol:
       if diff > 0:
          room = 1.0 - x
          mask = room > 1e-12
          if mask.any(): x[mask] += (diff / room[mask].sum()) * room[mask]
       else:
          mask = x > 1e-12
          if mask.any(): x[mask] += (diff / x[mask].sum()) * x[mask]
       x = np.clip(x,0.1? no clip 0,1)
    # Final exact summation adjustment if needed:
    diff = target - x.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-15:
       if diff >0:
          mask = x < 1.0 - 1e-12
       else: mask= x >1e-12
       if mask.any():
          x[mask] += diff
          x=np.clip(x,0,1)
          # if still, redistribute? one cell may exceed
          diff=target-x.sum()
          # small
    return x
```
But adding diff to all mask by equal diff may exceed bounds if diff not tiny. Bisection should make diff tiny. Good.
Potential if target outside [0,n] clip target. Not needed.

def count_full(h):
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0: return np.empty(0)
    if n == 1: return np.array([0.0])  # h(1-h)? Actually count = h*(1-h) maybe not zero. Wait zero shift count = h[0]*(1-h[0]). For n=1 target=0.5 h=0.5 count0=.25. So not zero.
```
For n=1, pos[0]=dot(h,omh)=0.25. full length1. General works.
```
    full = np.empty(2*n-1)
    omh = 1.0 - h
    full[n-1] = float(np.dot(h, omh))
    for s in range(1,n):
       L = n - s
       full[n+s] = float(np.dot(h[:L], omh[s:]))
       full[n-1-s] = float(np.dot(h[s:], omh[:L]))
    return full
```

`compute_c5_exact`:
```
def compute_c5_exact(h):
    n=len(h)
    if n==0: return np.inf
    dx=2.0/n
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')) * dx)
```
For n=1, correlate returns [h*(1-h)] *2 =>0.5 for h=0.5.

`smooth_fg` as above. For `_ALPHA` maybe global default 10. At top set 100.

`slsqp_step`:
```
def slsqp_step(h, n, alpha, maxiter):
    global _N, _TARGET, _ALPHA
    _N=n; _TARGET=0.5*n; _ALPHA=alpha
    h0=project_feasible(h,n)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':eq_fun,'jac':eq_jac}]
    try:
        res=minimize(smooth_fun, h0, jac=smooth_grad, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons,
                     options={'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'ftol':1e-11, 'gtol':1e-6})
        h1=project_feasible(res.x,n)
        c1=max_count(h1)*2.0/n
        return h1, c1
    except Exception:
        return h0, max_count(h0)*2.0/n
```
Possibly SLSQP may allocate large BFGS; for n=512 okay. If Memory? no.

      h1=project_feasible(res.x,n)
      c1=...
   except: return
```

`subgrad_polish`:
Need `grad_from_weights`.
```
def grad_from_weights(x, w):
  n=len(x); grad=np.zeros(n)
  w0=w[n-1]
  if w0: grad += w0*(1-2*x)
  for s in range(1,n):
    wp=w[n+s]
    if wp:
       L=n-s; grad[:L]+=wp*(1-x[s:s+L]); grad[s:s+L]-=wp*x[:L]
    wn=w[n-1-s]
    if wn:
       L=n-s; grad[s:s+L]+=wn*(1-x[:L]); grad[:L]-=wn*x[s:s+L]
  return grad
```
Use in smooth_fg? smooth_fg has weights normalized. Good.

Subgrad:
```
def subgrad_polish(h,n,deadline,maxiter=300,lr=0.5, active_tol=1e-4, topk=20):
  best_h=h.copy(); best_c=max_count(h)*2/n
  h=project_feasible(h,n)
  c=...
  for it in range(maxiter):
    if time.time()>deadline: break
    full=count_full(h); m=full.max()
    active = np.where(full >= m - active_tol)[0]
    if len(active) > topk and topk>0:
       idx = np.argsort(full[-?])? Use full[active], choose top topk by value: active[np.argsort(full[active])[::-1][:topk]]
    w=np.zeros_like(full); w[active]=1.0/len(active)
    g=grad_from_weights(h,w)
    gn=np.linalg.norm(g)
    if gn<1e-12: break
    base_step = lr / (gn + 1e-12)  # normalized direction
    accepted=False
    for factor in [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625,0.03125,0.015625]:
       step=base_step*factor
       y=project_feasible(h - step*g, n)
       cy=max_count(y)*2/n
       if cy < c - 1e-13:
          h=y; c=cy; accepted=True; break
    if not accepted:
       lr *=0.9
       if lr<1e-6: break
    else:
       if c < best_c: best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c
       lr = min(lr*1.05, 10.0)
  return best_h,best_c
```
But count_full for each line search expensive. Limit 500. active_tol in count units: differences maybe 1, use 0.01? For counts ~50, tol=1e-3? Use `active_tol = 1e-3 * max(1, m)`? If m~50, tol0.05. Or use top active shifts only: `topk=min(20, full.size)` largest count regardless of tol. That's better: gradient average of top 10/20 shifts. This explores near maxima.
```
K = min(20, full.size); active = np.argpartition(full, -K)[-K:]
w uniform
```
If plateau, argpartition arbitrary. Good. Use topk=10 maybe.

def random_start(n, rng, kind='fourier', parent=None, amp=0.2):
  x = (np.arange(n)+0.5)*2.0/n
  if parent is not None and kind in ('parent_noise','fourier_parent','binary_parent'):
      h = np.interp(x, (np.arange(parent.size)+0.5)*2.0/parent.size, parent)
  else:
      h = np.full(n,0.5)
  if kind == 'fourier' or 'parent_noise':
      for f in [1,2,3,4,6,8,12,16]:
          a = amp / (1.0+f)
          h += a*(rng.randn()*np.sin(np.pi*f*x) + rng.randn()*np.cos(np.pi*f*x))
  elif kind == 'sine': ...
  elif kind == 'binary':
      if parent is None: h fill 0.5? For binary, create zeros and choose n//2 indices set 1.
      else: h = (h>0.5).astype(float) and adjust count? Use rng.choice without replacement.
  if kind in ('binary','binary_parent'):
      h = np.zeros(n); idx = rng.choice(n, size=(n//2 if n even else (n//2)), replace=False); h[idx]=1; if n odd need sum .5? target n/2, if odd choose (n//2) ones and one 0.5? Simpler binary for even n only. We'll use even n=256/512.
  return project_feasible(h,n)
```
For parent_noise kind with parent, do not perturb too much.

`interp_to`:
```
def interp_to(h, n):
    m=len(h)
    if m==n: return project_feasible(h,n)
    xo=(np.arange(m)+0.5)*2.0/m
    xn=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2.0/n
    y=np.interp(xn, xo, h)
    return project_feasible(y,n)
```

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-15
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    best_h=None; best_c=np.inf; best_n=0

    # initial parent
    try:
       parent=np.asarray(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()
       if parent.size>0:
          parent=project_feasible(parent,len(parent))
          pc=compute_c5_exact(parent)
          best_h=parent; best_c=pc; best_n=parent.size
    except Exception:
       pass

    # If no best, create default n=256 fourier
    if best_h is None:
       h = random_start(256,rng,'fourier')
       c = max_count(h)*2/256
       best_h=h; best_c=c; best_n=256

    ...

`project_feasible`:
```
def project_feasible(y, target=None):
    y = np.array(y, dtype=float, copy=True)
    n = len(y)
    if n == 0: return y
    if target is None: target = 0.5 * n
    if target < 0: target = 0.0
    elif target > n: target = float(n)
    # If all within close? bisection:
    lo = float(np.min(y)) - 1.0
    hi = float(np.max(y)) + 1.0
    # If y already within bounds and sum close, can skip bisection?
    if y.min() >= -1e-12 and y.max() <= 1+1e-12 and abs(y.sum()-target) < 1e-7:
        x = np.clip(y,0.0,1.0)
    else:
        for _ in range(60):
            mid = 0.5*(lo+hi)
            s = float(np.clip(y-mid,0.0,1.0).sum())
            if s > target:
                lo = mid
            else:
                hi = mid
        x = np.clip(y - 0.5*(lo+hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(8):
        diff = float(target - x.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - x
            mask = room > 1e-15
            if mask.any():
                x[mask] += (diff / float(room[mask].sum())) * room[mask]
        else:
            mask = x > 1e-15
            if mask.any():
                x[mask] += (diff / float(x[mask].sum())) * x[mask]
        x = np.clip(x,0.0,1.0)
    return x
```
If y has NaN? no.

`count_full`:
```
def count_full(h):
    n = len(h)
    full = np.empty(2*n-1, dtype=float)
    omh = 1.0 - h
    full[n-1] = float(np.dot(h, omh))
    for s in range(1,n):
        L = n - s
        full[n+s] = float(np.dot(h[:L], omh[s:]))
        full[n-1-s] = float(np.dot(h[s:], omh[:L]))
    return full
```
If n=0? not used. If n=1, loop skip full[0]=dot. OK.

`max_count`:
```
def max_count(h):
    if len(h)==0: return np.inf
    return float(np.max(count_full(h)))
```

`grad_from_weights`:
```
def grad_from_weights(x, w):
    n=len(x)
    g=np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if n==1:
        w0=float(w[0]) if len(w)>0 else 0.0
        g[0] += w0*(1.0 - 2.0*x[0])
        return g
    w0=float(w[n-1])
    if w0 != 0.0:
        g += w0*(1.0 - 2.0*x)
    for s in range(1,n):
        wp=float(w[n+s])
        if wp != 0.0:
            L=n-s
            g[:L] += wp*(1.0 - x[s:s+L])
            g[s:s+L] -= wp*x[:L]
        wn=float(w[n-1-s])
        if wn != 0.0:
            L=n-s
            g[s:s+L] += wn*(1.0 - x[:L])
            g[:L] -= wn*x[s:s+L]
    return g
```
For n=1, full len1; w[0]. OK.

`smooth_fun`, `smooth_grad` as wrappers.

`pen_fun`, `pen_grad`:
```
def pen_fun(x):
    f,g = smooth_fg(x)
    diff = float(np.sum(x) - _TARGET)
    return f + 0.5*_PEN*diff*diff
def pen_grad(x):
    f,g = smooth_fg(x)
    diff = float(np.sum(x) - _TARGET)
    if abs(diff) < 1e-15 or _PEN == 0: return g
    return g + _PEN*diff*np.ones(len(x))
```
Double. To reduce, could compute in `pen_grad` `f,g` and return; okay.

```
def optimize_path(h, n, deadline, alpha_list, maxiter, do_lbfgs=True, lbfgs_iters=300, do_subgrad=False, subgrad_iters=200):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c = max_count(h) * (2.0 / n)
    for alpha in alpha_list:
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        h2, c2 = slsqp_step(h, n, alpha, maxiter)
        if c2 < c:
            h = h2; c = c2
    if do_lbfgs and time.time() < deadline:
        alpha = float(alpha_list[-1]) if len(alpha_list) else 1000.0
        h2, c2 = lbfgs_step(h, n, alpha, lbfgs_iters, 500.0)
        if c2 < c: h=h2;c=c2
    if do_subgrad and time.time() < deadline:
        h, c = subgrad_polish(h, n, deadline, subgrad_iters)
    return h,c
```
Could run SLSQP multiple times even if no improvement to escape? The result might be same; okay.

`subgrad_polish` top-level:
```
def subgrad_polish(h,n,deadline,maxiter=200,lr=1.0,topk=12):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    c=max_count(h)*2.0/n
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c
    for it in range(maxiter):
       if time.time() > deadline: break
       full=count_full(h)
       m=float(np.max(full))
       K = min(topk, len(full))
       active = np.argpartition(full, len(full)-K)[-K:] if K < len(full) else np.arange(len(full))
       w=np.zeros_like(full); w[active] = 1.0/K
       g=grad_from_weights(h,w)
       gn=float(np.linalg.norm(g))
       if gn < 1e-12: break
       base = lr / gn
       accepted=False
       for fac in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625,0.03125):
           y=project_feasible(h - base*fac*g, n)
           cy=max_count(y)*2.0/n
           if cy < c - 1e-13:
               h=y; c=cy; accepted=True; break
       if not accepted:
           lr *= 0.9
           if lr < 1e-6: break
       else:
           if c < best_c: best_c=c; best_h=h.copy()
           lr = min(lr*1.05, 10.0)
    return best_h,best_c
```
`np.argpartition(full, len(full)-K)`: if K==len, avoid. For K=12, works. Need if full length 0 no.

But `max_count(y)` recomputes count_full each line; each count includes full. For subgrad 200*6*512^2 ~318M, okay maybe but could be slow. Limit subgrad_iters maybe 100.

Alternatively line search accept if `cy < c` even tiny; okay.

def random_start(n, rng, kind='fourier', parent=None, amp=0.2):
    n=int(n); x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)*2.0/n
    h=np.full(n,0.5)
    if parent is not None:
       if kind in ('parent_fourier','parent_noise'):
          m=len(parent); xo=(np.arange(m)+0.5)*2.0/m; h=np.interp(x,xo,parent)
       elif kind in ('binary_parent',):
          m=len(parent); ...; h = binary_from_values(np.interp(x,xo,parent), n)
    if kind == 'fourier':
       freqs=[1,2,3,4,6,8,12,16]
       for f in freqs:
           h += (amp/(1.0+f))*(rng.randn()*np.sin(np.pi*f*x)+rng.randn()*np.cos(np.pi*f*x))
    elif kind == 'parent_fourier':
       freqs=[1,2,3,4,6,8]
       for f ...
    elif kind == 'binary' or kind == 'binary_parent':
       # from h value rank
       order=np.argsort(h)[::-1]
       h=np.zeros(n)
       target=n//2
       h[order[:target]]=1.0
       # if n odd? not used; if odd target n//2 gives sum floor, need add 0.5? For even fine.
    elif kind == 'ramp':
       h = (np.arange(n)+0.5)/float(n) # sum n/2, but ramp max high
    return project_feasible(h,n)
```
For parent_noise and amp tiny, use amp maybe 0.05.

Let's consider implementing a **simple projected gradient FISTA** to polish high n better than L-BFGS:
```
def fista_smooth(h,n,deadline,maxiter,alpha):
  set _ALPHA
  x=project; y=x; t=1.0; best...
  for it:
    f,g=smooth_fg(y)
    gradnorm...
    # set step = 1/(it+1+L) maybe L= n? Use L= max(1, n/10)
    step = 1.0 / (L + it*?)
    x_new = project(y - step*g, n)
    t_new = 0.5*(1+sqrt(1+4*t*t))
    y = x_new + ((t-1)/t_new)*(x_new - x)
    project y
    evaluate c; update best
```

Now, code for `optimize_path` with `do_slsqp` variable. Make sure `alpha_list` not empty; for lbfgs use last.

`optimize_path`:
```
def optimize_path(h, n, deadline, alpha_list, maxiter, do_slsqp=True, do_lbfgs=True, lbfgs_iters=300, do_subgrad=False, subgrad_iters=100, subgrad_lr=1.0):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c = max_count(h) * (2.0 / n)
    if do_slsqp:
        for alpha in alpha_list:
            if time.time() > deadline: break
            h2, c2 = slsqp_step(h, n, alpha, maxiter)
            if c2 < c:
                h = h2; c = c2
    if do_lbfgs and time.time() < deadline:
        alpha = float(alpha_list[-1]) if len(alpha_list) > 0 else 1000.0
        h2, c2 = lbfgs_step(h, n, alpha, lbfgs_iters, 1000.0)
        if c2 < c: h=h2;c=c2
    if do_subgrad and time.time() < deadline:
        h, c = subgrad_polish(h, n, deadline, subgrad_iters, subgrad_lr)
    return h, c
```
If do_slsqp False, still want LBFGS with alpha maybe from list.

Top-level globals `_CACHE_X=None, _CACHE_F=None, _CACHE_G=None`. In `smooth_fg(x)`, we compute and store? But then `smooth_fun` and `smooth_grad` call `smooth_fg`, which would store. Let's implement a simple top-level `_cache_same(a,b)`:
```
def fg_cache_same(a,b):
    if a is None: return False
    if len(a) != len(b): return False
    if a is b: return True
    if a[0] != b[0] or a[-1] != b[-1] or a[1:-1].sum() != b[1:-1].sum(): return False
    # maybe exact compare if n small
    if len(a) <= 1024:
       return np.array_equal(a,b)
    return True
```

    hB,cB=optimize_path(startB,nB,dlB,[20,80,300,1200,5000],itB,do_slsqp=True,do_lbfgs=True,lbfgs_iters=2*itB,do_subgrad=False)
    update
```
If no best? fallback random.

if time.time() < dlC - 100 and best_h is not None:

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

# Globals used by top-level optimizer callbacks.
_N = 0
_TARGET = 0.0
_ALPHA = 100.0
_PEN = 1000.0

# Simple caches so that fun/jac pairs need not recompute the O(n^2) correlation.
_FG_X = None
_FG_F = 0.0
_FG_G = None

_PEN_X = None
_PEN_F = 0.0
_PEN_G = None


def project_feasible(y, target=None):
    """Project y to [0,1]^n with sum(target)."""
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
    n = len(y)
    if n == 0:
        return y.copy()
    if target is None:
        target = 0.5 * n
    target = float(np.clip(target, 0.0, float(n)))

    # If already close and boxed, only do tiny correction.
    if y.min() >= -1e-12 and y.max() <= 1.0 + 1e-12 and abs(float(y.sum()) - target) < 1e-7:
        x = np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0)
    else:
        lo = float(np.min(y)) - 1.0
        hi = float(np.max(y)) + 1.0
        for _ in range(60):
            mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
            s = float(np.clip(y - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum())
            if s > target:
                lo = mid
            else:
                hi = mid
        x = np.clip(y - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)

    # Small proportional corrections to make the sum very accurate.
    for _ in range(10):
        diff = float(target - x.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - x
            mask = room > 1e-15
            if mask.any():
                x[mask] += (diff / float(room[mask].sum())) * room[mask]
        else:
            mask = x > 1e-15
            if mask.any():
                x[mask] += (diff / float(x[mask].sum())) * x[mask]
        x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0)
    return x


def count_full(h):
    """
    Return the unweighted overlap counts for all finite shifts:
      full[n-1] = sum_i h_i(1-h_i)
      positive shift s: sum_i h_i(1-h_{i+s})
      negative shift -s: sum_i h_{i+s}(1-h_i)
    """
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=float)
    full = np.empty(2 * n - 1, dtype=float)
    omh = 1.0 - h
    full[n - 1] = float(np.dot(h, omh))
    for s in range(1, n):
        L = n - s
        full[n + s] = float(np.dot(h[:L], omh[s:]))
        full[n - 1 - s] = float(np.dot(h[s:], omh[:L]))
    return full


def max_count(h):
    """Maximum overlap count (not multiplied by dx)."""
    if len(h) == 0:
        return np.inf
    return float(np.max(count_full(h)))


def compute_c5_exact(h):
    """Exact task objective using the evaluator's definition."""
    n = len(h)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    dx = 2.0 / n
    c = np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")) * dx
    return float(c)


def grad_from_weights(x, w):
    """
    Gradient of the weighted sum of overlap counts.
    w is length 2*n-1, ordered like count_full.
    """
    n = len(x)
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if n == 0:
        return g
    if n == 1:
        w0 = float(w[0]) if len(w) > 0 else 0.0
        g[0] += w0 * (1.0 - 2.0 * x[0])
        return g

    w0 = float(w[n - 1])
    if w0 != 0.0:
        g += w0 * (1.0 - 2.0 * x)

    for s in range(1, n):
        wp = float(w[n + s])
        if wp != 0.0:
            L = n - s
            g[:L] += wp * (1.0 - x[s:s + L])
            g[s:s + L] -= wp * x[:L]

        wn = float(w[n - 1 - s])
        if wn != 0.0:
            L = n - s
            g[s:s + L] += wn * (1.0 - x[:L])
            g[:L] -= wn * x[s:s + L]
    return g


def smooth_fg_compute(x):
    """
    Smooth max over overlap counts:
      F = m + alpha^{-1} log sum exp(alpha(c_k - m)).
    Returns (F, gradient).
    """
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    n = len(x)
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0, x.copy()

    full = count_full(x)
    m = float(np.max(full))
    alpha = float(_ALPHA)

    if alpha <= 1.0:
        f = m
        w = np.zeros_like(full)
        mask = full >= (m - 1e-12)
        k = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
        if k > 0:
            w[mask] = 1.0 / k
        else:
            w[int(np.argmax(full))] = 1.0
    else:
        sh = alpha * (full - m)
        sm = float(np.max(sh))
        with np.errstate(under="ignore", over="ignore"):
            ez = np.exp(sh - sm)
        S = float(np.sum(ez))
        if S <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(S):
            idx = int(np.argmax(full))
            w = np.zeros_like(full)
            w[idx] = 1.0
            f = m
        else:
            f = m + np.log(S) / alpha
            w = ez / S

    g = grad_from_weights(x, w)
    return f, g


def smooth_fun(x):
    global _FG_X, _FG_F, _FG_G
    if _FG_X is not None and len(x) == len(_FG_X) and np.array_equal(x, _FG_X):
        return _FG_F
    f, g = smooth_fg_compute(x)
    _FG_X = np.array(x, dtype=float, copy=True)
    _FG_F = f
    _FG_G = np.array(g, dtype=float, copy=True)
    return f


def smooth_grad(x):
    global _FG_X, _FG_F, _FG_G
    if _FG_X is not None and len(x) == len(_FG_X) and np.array_equal(x, _FG_X):
        return _FG_G
    f, g = smooth_fg_compute(x)
    _FG_X = np.array(x, dtype=float, copy=True)
    _FG_F = f
    _FG_G = np.array(g, dtype=float, copy=True)
    return _FG_G


def pen_fg_compute(x):
    f, g = smooth_fg_compute(x)
    diff = float(np.sum(x) - _TARGET)
    f = f + 0.5 * _PEN * diff * diff
    g = g + _PEN * diff * np.ones(len(x), dtype=float)
    return f, g


def pen_fun(x):
    global _PEN_X, _PEN_F, _PEN_G
    if _PEN_X is not None and len(x) == len(_PEN_X) and np.array_equal(x, _PEN_X):
        return _PEN_F
    f, g = pen_fg_compute(x)
    _PEN_X = np.array(x, dtype=float, copy=True)
    _PEN_F = f
    _PEN_G = np.array(g, dtype=float, copy=True)
    return f


def pen_grad(x):
    global _PEN_X, _PEN_F, _PEN_G
    if _PEN_X is not None and len(x) == len(_PEN_X) and np.array_equal(x, _PEN_X):
        return _PEN_G
    f, g = pen_fg_compute(x)
    _PEN_X = np.array(x, dtype=float, copy=True)
    _PEN_F = f
    _PEN_G = np.array(g, dtype=float, copy=True)
    return _PEN_G


def eq_fun(x):
    return float(np.sum(x) - _TARGET)


def eq_jac(x):
    return np.ones(_N, dtype=float)


def slsqp_step(h, n, alpha, maxiter):
    global _N, _TARGET, _ALPHA

    h0 = project_feasible(np.asarray(h, dtype=float))
    c0 = max_count(h0) * (2.0 / n)

    _N = int(n)
    _TARGET = 0.5 * float(n)
    _ALPHA = float(alpha)

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    constraints = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': eq_fun, 'jac': eq_jac}]
    try:
        res = minimize(
            smooth_fun,
            h0,
            jac=smooth_grad,
            method='SLSQP',
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=constraints,
            options={
                'maxiter': int(max(5, maxiter)),
                'ftol': 1e-10,
                'gtol': 1e-5,
            }
        )
        if res.x is None:
            return h0, c0
        h1 = project_feasible(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float))
        c1 = max_count(h1) * (2.0 / n)
        if np.isfinite(c1) and c1 < c0:
            return h1, c1
        return h0, c0
    except Exception:
        return h0, c0


def lbfgs_step(h, n, alpha, maxiter, pen):
    global _N, _TARGET, _ALPHA, _PEN

    h0 = project_feasible(np.asarray(h, dtype=float))
    c0 = max_count(h0) * (2.0 / n)

    _N = int(n)
    _TARGET = 0.5 * float(n)
    _ALPHA = float(alpha)
    _PEN = float(pen)

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    try:
        res = minimize(
            pen_fun,
            h0,
            jac=pen_grad,
            method='L-BFGS-B',
            bounds=bounds,
            options={
                'maxiter': int(max(5, maxiter)),
                'ftol': 1e-10,
                'gtol': 1e-5,
                'maxls': 50,
            }
        )
        if res.x is None:
            return h0, c0
        h1 = project_feasible(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float))
        c1 = max_count(h1) * (2.0 / n)
        if np.isfinite(c1) and c1 < c0:
            return h1, c1
        return h0, c0
    except Exception:
        return h0, c0


def subgrad_polish(h, n, deadline, max_iter=100, lr=1.0, topk=12):
    """A concise top-shift subgradient polish for the true max."""
    h = project_feasible(np.asarray(h, dtype=float))
    n = len(h)
    c = max_count(h) * (2.0 / n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c

    for _ in range(int(max_iter)):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        full = count_full(h)
        sz = len(full)
        if sz == 0:
            break
        K = int(max(1, min(topk, sz)))
        if K < sz:
            active = np.argpartition(full, -K)[-K:]
        else:
            active = np.arange(sz)
        w = np.zeros(sz, dtype=float)
        w[active] = 1.0 / float(K)

        g = grad_from_weights(h, w)
        gn = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
        if gn < 1e-13:
            break

        base = lr / gn
        accepted = False
        for fac in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625, 0.03125):
            y = project_feasible(h - base * fac * g)
            cy = max_count(y) * (2.0 / n)
            if np.isfinite(cy) and cy < c - 1e-13:
                h = y
                c = cy
                accepted = True
                break

        if not accepted:
            lr *= 0.88
            if lr < 1e-7:
                break
        else:
            if c < best_c:
                best_c = c
                best_h = h.copy()
            lr = min(lr * 1.04, 12.0)

    return best_h, best_c


def interp_to(h, n):
    m = len(h)
    if m == n:
        return project_feasible(np.asarray(h, dtype=float))
    xo = (np.arange(m) + 0.5) * (2.0 / m)
    xn = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    y = np.interp(xn, xo, np.asarray(h, dtype=float))
    return project_feasible(y)


def random_start(n, rng, kind="fourier", parent=None, amp=0.2):
    n = int(n)
    x = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) * (2.0 / n)
    h = np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float)

    if parent is not None and kind in ("parent_fourier", "parent_noise", "binary_parent"):
        m = len(parent)
        if m == n:
            h = np.asarray(parent, dtype=float).copy()
        else:
            xo = (np.arange(m) + 0.5) * (2.0 / m)
            h = np.interp(x, xo, np.asarray(parent, dtype=float))

    if kind == "binary":
        h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        target = n // 2
        idx = rng.choice(n, size=target, replace=False)
        h[idx] = 1.0
        return project_feasible(h)

    if kind == "binary_parent":
        order = np.argsort(h)[::-1]
        h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        target = n // 2
        h[order[:target]] = 1.0
        return project_feasible(h)

    if kind in ("fourier", "parent_fourier", "parent_noise"):
        if kind == "parent_noise":
            freqs = [2, 3, 4, 6, 8]
            amp_eff = amp * 0.75
        else:
            freqs = [1, 2, 3, 4, 6, 8, 12, 16]
            amp_eff = amp
        for f in freqs:
           .h += (amp_eff / (1.0 + float(f))) * (
                rng.randn() * np.sin(np.pi * f * x) +
                rng.randn() * np.cos(np.pi * f * x)
            )
    elif kind == "ramp":
        h = (np.arange(n) + 0.5) / float(n)

    return project_feasible(h)


def optimize_path(h, n, deadline, alpha_list, maxiter,
                  do_slsqp=True, do_lbfgs=True, lbfgs_iters=300,
                  do_subgrad=False, subgrad_iters=100, subgrad_lr=1.0):
    h = project_feasible(np.asarray(h, dtype=float))
    n = len(h)
    c = max_count(h) * (2.0 / n)

    if do_slsqp:
        for alpha in alpha_list:
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            h2, c2 = slsqp_step(h, n, alpha, maxiter)
            if c2 < c:
                h = h2
                c = c2

    if do_lbfgs and time.time() < deadline:
        alpha = float(alpha_list[-1]) if len(alpha_list) > 0 else 1000.0
        h2, c2 = lbfgs_step(h, n, alpha, lbfgs_iters, 1000.0)
        if c2 < c:
            h = h2
            c = c2

    if do_subgrad and time.time() < deadline:
        h, c = subgrad_polish(h, n, deadline, max_iter=subgrad_iters, lr=subgrad_lr, topk=12)

    return h, c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    total = float(max(60.0, budget_s))
    reserve = 20.0
    end = t0 + max(30.0, total - reserve)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    best_h = None
    best_c = np.inf
    best_n = 0

    # Use the supplied parent directly if available.
    try:
        parent = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        if parent.size > 0:
            parent = project_feasible(parent)
            pc = compute_c5_exact(parent)
            if np.isfinite(pc):
                best_h = parent.copy()
                best_c = pc
                best_n = int(parent.size)
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Fallback if no parent is available.
    if best_h is None:
        n = 256
        h = random_start(n, rng, "fourier", amp=0.35)
        c = max_count(h) * (2.0 / float(n))
        best_h = h
        best_c = c
        best_n = n

    # ---------- Phase A: polish at the parent/resolution near the parent ----------
    nA = best_n if best_n >= 64 else 256
    dlA = t0 + min(220.0, 0.20 * (total - reserve))
    if time.time() < dlA:
        startA = best_h if len(best_h) == nA else interp_to(best_h, nA)
        itA = max(25, min(120, int(25000.0 / max(1, nA))))
        hA, cA = optimize_path(
            startA, nA, dlA,
            [20.0, 80.0, 300.0, 1200.0],
            itA,
            do_slsqp=True,
            do_lbfgs=True,
            lbfgs_iters=max(80, 2 * itA),
            do_subgrad=True,
            subgrad_iters=70
        )
        cAe = compute_c5_exact(hA)
        if np.isfinite(cAe) and cAe < best_c:
            best_h = hA.copy()
            best_c = float(cAe)
            best_n = int(len(hA))

    # ---------- Phase B1: try a mid-resolution 384 ----------
    nB = 384
    dlB = t0 + min(640.0, 0.56 * (total - reserve))
    if time.time() < dlB - 80:
        startB = interp_to(best_h, nB)
        itB = max(25, min(95, int(24000.0 / nB)))
        hB, cB = optimize_path(
            startB, nB, dlB,
            [20.0, 80.0, 300.0, 1200.0, 5000.0],
            itB,
            do_slsqp=True,
            do_lbfgs=True,
            lbfgs_iters=max(100, 2 * itB),
            do_subgrad=False
        )
        cBe = compute_c5_exact(hB)
        if np.isfinite(cBe) and cBe < best_c:
            best_h = hB.copy()
            best_c = float(cBe)
            best_n = int(len(hB))

    # ---------- Phase B2: main work at n=512, plus diverse starts ----------
    nC = 512
    dlC = t0 + min(900.0, 0.80 * (total - reserve))
    if time.time() < dlC - 120:
        startC = interp_to(best_h, nC)
        itC = max(20, min(85, int(22000.0 / nC)))
        hC, cC = optimize_path(
            startC, nC, dlC,
            [30.0, 120.0, 500.0, 2000.0, 8000.0],
            itC,
            do_slsqp=True,
            do_lbfgs=True,
            lbfgs_iters=max(150, 2 * itC),
            do_subgrad=True,
            subgrad_iters=80
        )
        cCe = compute_c5_exact(hC)
        if np.isfinite(cCe) and cCe < best_c:
            best_h = hC.copy()
            best_c = float(cCe)
            best_n = int(len(hC))

    # Random smooth start at 512.
    if time.time() < dlC - 120:
        sR = random_start(nC, rng, "fourier", amp=0.35)
        hR, cR = optimize_path(
            sR, nC, dlC,
            [100.0, 500.0, 2000.0],
            45,
            do_slsqp=False,
            do_lbfgs=True,
            lbfgs_iters=220,
            do_subgrad=True,
            subgrad_iters=60
        )
        cRe = compute_c5_exact(hR)
        if np.isfinite(cRe) and cRe < best_c:
            best_h = hR.copy()
            best_c = float(cRe)
            best_n = int(len(hR))

    # Binary / near-binary parent-derived start at 512.
    if time.time() < dlC - 80:
        kind = "binary_parent" if best_h is not None else "binary"
        par = best_h if best_h is not None else None
        sBin = random_start(nC, rng, kind, parent=par, amp=0.0)
        hBin, cBin = optimize_path(
            sBin, nC, dlC,
            [50.0, 200.0, 800.0],
            35,
            do_slsqp=False,
            do_lbfgs=True,
            lbfgs_iters=180,
            do_subgrad=True,
            subgrad_iters=50
        )
        cBinE = compute_c5_exact(hBin)
        if np.isfinite(cBinE) and cBinE < best_c:
            best_h = hBin.copy()
            best_c = float(cBinE)
            best_n = int(len(hBin))

    # ---------- Final polish ----------
    if best_h is not None and time.time() < end - 20:
        nF = int(best_n)
        itF = max(20, min(80, int(18000.0 / max(1, nF))))
        do_slsqp_final = (nF <= 700)
        hF, cF = optimize_path(
            project_feasible(best_h), nF, end - 18,
            [500.0, 2000.0, 10000.0],
            itF,
            do_slsqp=do_slsqp_final,
            do_lbfgs=True,
            lbfgs_iters=260,
            do_subgrad=True,
            subgrad_iters=160
        )
        cFe = compute_c5_exact(hF)
        if np.isfinite(cFe) and cFe < best_c:
            best_h = hF.copy()
            best_c = float(cFe)
            best_n = int(len(hF))

    # Try one modest upsample if there is enough time.  This can reduce
    # discretization error if the solution is smooth.
    if best_h is not None and int(best_n) < 1024 and time.time() < end - 70:
        nU = 2 * int(best_n) if int(best_n) < 512 else 1024
        if nU >= 64:
            sU = interp_to(best_h, nU)
            hU, cU = optimize_path(
                sU, nU, end - 35,
                [1000.0, 5000.0],
                30,
                do_slsqp=False,
                do_lbfgs=True,
                lbfgs_iters=220,
                do_subgrad=True,
                subgrad_iters=70
            )
            cUe = compute_c5_exact(hU)
            if np.isfinite(cUe) and cUe < best_c:
                best_h = hU.copy()
                best_c = float(cUe)
                best_n = int(len(hU))

    # A small noisy restart from the best point can escape a very shallow local set.
    if best_h is not None and time.time() < end - 25:
        nN = int(best_n)
        amp = 0.03
        sN = project_feasible(np.asarray(best_h, dtype=float) + amp * rng.randn(nN))
        hN, cN = optimize_path(
            sN, nN, end - 10,
            [2000.0, 10000.0],
            25,
            do_slsqp=False,
            do_lbfgs=True,
            lbfgs_iters=180,
            do_subgrad=True,
            subgrad_iters=50
        )
        cNe = compute_c5_exact(hN)
        if np.isfinite(cNe) and cNe < best_c:
            best_h = hN.copy()
            best_c = float(cNe)
            best_n = int(len(hN))

    # Fallback safety.
    if best_h is None or not np.isfinite(best_c):
        n = 256
        h = project_feasible(np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float))
        best_h = h
        best_n = n
        best_c = compute_c5_exact(h)

    # Final feasibility guard, avoiding unnecessary perturbation.
    target = 0.5 * float(best_n)
    if abs(float(np.sum(best_h)) - target) > 1e-10:
        best_h = project_feasible(np.asarray(best_h, dtype=float))

    final_c = compute_c5_exact(best_h)
    if not np.isfinite(final_c):
        final_c = float(best_c)

    return np.asarray(best_h, dtype=float), float(final_c), int(best_n)
```
